# #333 review, findings 266-530: dispositions (head ded52c8fb, branch hep/333-review-q2)

Base for "pre-existing" is main f91058158 (`anima/main`). "fix" = done in this branch; "fix (test, red→green shown)" = the strengthened row ran green, red under the named mutant, green again, and the old test stayed green under the same mutant (counts are passed/failed of the suite). "PROPOSED" = the exact proposal is under "Proposed test fixes"; it needs a browser, a pinned library, a base checkout, or an owner call, or I didn't get to it. "fix + pre-existing" = the wrong comment/doc was fixed and main carried the same text.

## Also spotted (not in the bot's list; not fixed unless noted)

- Merge with hep/333-review-q1: `git merge-tree` shows content conflicts in spec/PROTOCOL.md, test/client/flight-test.ts, test/gate/hygiene-check.ts, test/protocol/scale-check.ts and test/server/knockdown-test.ts (both branches edited the same lines; 42 files touched by both).
- test/shared/physconst-test.ts A3 (#530): the occupant's logged p is not rounded to 3 dp (`1.7000000000000002`); the row only checks a JSON round trip. Behaviour question for the owner (main's label claimed 3 dp too).
- test/client/reachlive-test.ts: three other count rows ("…and it actually ran on every rig", "…the sweep actually ran", "…it actually ran") are red locally with the 12-rig asset-lib; they assume the gate's pinned library. Not this branch's doing; run with `run.ts --library` to judge.
- test/server/knockdown-test.ts "released with a nail: the agent's sim HANGS the body" is the only row that catches a dropped `this.pins.set` before #331's new setup row.
- test/protocol/shove-check.ts --control (#297): at base e2238c07 S0 fails `no 'crate'` (no lab catalogue), so the control can't run its one green row there.
- mcpl/agent.ts:382-384 (the onMedia doc): its frame lists left out voice-consent, surface-transition, voice-service and voice-moderated outbound, and sfu-pos and voice-consent inbound (code :698-710, :2611-2612). Fixed.
- test/gate/suites.ts:482: two docblocks were stacked on outputKeeper; the first was failLines'. Moved it to failLines (:495). Fixed.
- test/server/settled-pose-test.ts:28: the mirror doesn't drop `t`/`seq`, which server.ts:97 does (pre-existing, main same). This is the drift the file says it prevents; covered by the #301 proposal.
- test/gate/ungated.json:22: the flight-headless reason gives only the VRM store; the suite also needs the absolute rig.json path at test/shared/flight-headless-test.ts:122 (see #303).
- test/client/picture-editor-probe.ts:126: the D row name "nothing was sent" overstates the same way as the header (see #271).
- tools/world.ts:93: the CLI's "(writes nothing; prints the report and its hash)" is false on a host with no custody key (runtime string, see #310).
- test/protocol/shiplist-check.ts R1: entries that error in the planner are silently skipped (:223); worth a printed count (see #295).
- test/client/tts-test.ts:297: the "queue is bounded (or draining), not runaway" row (`q < 1000` after 12 lines) can't fail, and client/lib/audio/tts.js:273 has no bound. Same on main (tools/tts-test.ts:283). Rename the row or drop it.
- server/oldworlds.ts:43 and tools/world.ts:47: two copies of the CLI name grammar (the latter's comment points at the former). They should share one export.
- test/gate/hygiene-check.ts:36: the old header left bench/ out of the ungated list, but the scan includes it (:187). Fixed together with #321.
- deploy/deploy-vps.sh:53: the refusal message "(its header says what goes in it)" assumes every unit has a header that documents its env file. Not checked.
- mcpl/agent.ts:594: the 8 s join timer rejects with `this.joinRefused` after the 4005/4010 branch already called close(). Harmless, since the promise has already settled.
- test/gate/suites.ts:436: "run-client.ts runs every tools/*-test file" was stale (tools/ → test/). FIXED: it now says `test/**/*-test`.
- mcpl/agent.ts:2088-2089 says "a routed walk's [corners] … and its end count within that". That's true, but WALKER.md said otherwise until #358.
- shared/protocol/componentspec.js:477-483 physics-volume help/docs still show `modes` as alternatives, not a list. It's wire text (see #365).
- test/client/reexport-binding-test.mjs:10 run line says `node`. That's a dup of q1 #178 (fixed on the q1 branch, not in this worktree).
- server/routes.ts:1050 /shared/ is also `no-store` + ETag, the same as #372, and it's the "control" the 2d3b2a468 test compares against.
- shared/protocol/authority/writes.js:432: a second `spend(Infinity)` call that poisons WORK_METER (#357).
- test/protocol/vocab-check.ts:133 INCIDENTS filter: already in q1's Also spotted.
- E1 (names-check.ts:547) calls `noteActs` through optional chaining (`noteActs?.`) on a private method. If `ag2.senses` is missing, the call silently does nothing, so check that `heard` is ever non-empty before strengthening (#390).

- docs/contributing/TOOL-LADDER.md:30: "the row: { name, title, description, inputSchema, annotations } in TOOL_ROWS". title and annotations come from the separate ANNOTATIONS table (mcpl/tools.ts:118-161), not the row.
- shared/flora-presets.js:4 ("GR2 holds the two equal") is true only after the #465 proposal lands.
- test/protocol/refusedjoin-check.ts:28, test/protocol/build-probe.ts:3, test/client/xr-plate-card-probe.mjs:6, test/client/xr-pointer-probe.mjs:7 still cite code/scripts/eido-probe.sh, a host script that isn't in this repo (same class as #495; not edited).

## Proposed test fixes, not applied (each with the mutation that should turn it red)

Applied ones are in the table (marked "red→green shown"). 297 and 530 were tried and reverted (see their rows). The rest, as the triage agents wrote them; "Unverified" means nobody has run it.

- **266** test/client/foliage-probe.mjs:122. Change `r.hits.length > 0 && r.hits.every(...)` to `r.hits.length === 6 && r.hits.every(...)`. The old row stays green (3 hits, no core) and the new one goes red. Needs a browser.
- **268** test/client/library-badge-probe.mjs, after :104 add `check('every card is its catalog entry, in order (by name)', !mism.some((m) => /^card \d+ is /.test(m)), mism.filter((m) => /^card \d+ is /.test(m)).slice(0, 3));`. Caveat: :85 says display names truncate at 48 chars, so :89 may need `c.name === h.name \|\| h.name.startsWith(c.name.replace(/…$/, ''))` first (the truncation format is unverified). Mutant (probe-side): before the loop, `[r.cards[2], r.cards[3]] = [r.cards[3], r.cards[2]]`. The old checks stay green (both cards are skipped) and the new one goes red. Needs a browser.
- **271** test/client/picture-editor-probe.ts. Before `pg.fill` (:122) add `await pg.evaluate(() => { const s = WebSocket.prototype.send; (globalThis as any).__sent = []; WebSocket.prototype.send = function (d: any) { (globalThis as any).__sent.push(String(d)); return s.call(this, d); }; });`. After seqAfter add `const sent = await pg.evaluate(() => (globalThis as any).__sent.filter((d: string) => /picture/.test(d)).length);` and require `sent === 0` in the :126 check. Unverified that net sends through WebSocket.prototype.send. Needs a browser.

- **276** test/client/xr-panel-drag-probe.mjs. After the release (:110), `const cbScrolled = sb ? sb.scrollTop - cbS0 : null;`. Return it, and require `r.cbScrolled >= 40` in :143. Mutant: in the dom-quads drag handler, skip the scrollTop write when the press element is an input. The old row stays green (dragging is set, no toggle). Caveat: if the checkbox's panel can't scroll, rename the row to "is a drag and never toggles it" instead.
- **286** test/protocol/describe-particles-check.ts:362-369. Add `let ran = 0;`, then `ran++` after the `--only` skip, and print `${ran - bad} passed, ${bad} failed`. # #333 review, findings 531-795: dispositions (head ded52c8fb, branch hep/333-review-q3)

Base for "pre-existing" is main f91058158 (`anima/main`). The labels mean the same as in q1/q2. "fix (test, red→green shown)": the strengthened row ran green, then red under the named mutant (the old row green under the same mutant), then green again. Counts are passed/failed of the suite. "PROPOSED": not applied, for the reason in the row.

Commits (on e50d693b1): c081de5aa mention-rx product one-liner + its rows (#737) · a73558ff1 tests that couldn't fail · 51650d77f test row labels and printed lines (strings only, droppable) · 2e88e4a0d nonumber-check and paths-check green again (part-1 slips, see Also spotted) · 3b122ae62 comments and docs.

Triage: two read-only helpers drafted rows and exact edits (531-662, 663-795). I applied, verified and ran everything, and rewrote the rows whose outcome changed.

Same row on main tools/framebudget-test.mjs:83. ## Also spotted (not in the bot's list; not fixed unless noted)

- **Two tree scanners were red at this lane's start, from earlier fixes on this branch (not in ded52c8fb). FIXED in 2e88e4a0d.** nonumber-check: docs/contributing/GATE-MANIFEST.md:163 named the fork's branch by its number (71f751d43); it now says "the fork's protocol branch". paths-check P3: test/protocol/build-check.ts:91's old-tree fallback `client/lib/newscript.js` (5864a479b) has an ALLOW row now, like roles-check's. A third P3 hit, test/client/grass-quality-test.ts:57, came from this lane's own #782 edit and was reworded. All three suites pass now (nonumber exit 0, paths 8/0, hygiene 33/0).
- test/services/captionbot-test.ts: the first run (with the borrowed voice-kit) had H "…under a session that follows the old one, so they drain first" and "rotateSession refuses to go backwards" red (44/2). The next three runs were 46/0 or 45/1 (the mutant row only), so H looks timing-flaky. Not investigated.
- The #536 draft put a trailing `// comment` inside a one-line `C(...)` call, which commented out the rest of the call (a known "trailing // eats the line" class). To catch any repeat, every changed .js/.ts/.mjs was transpiled at HEAD and at the worktree, comment-stripped. Only the 24 files with string/test/product commits differ.
- test/protocol/r9-check.ts:25 imports `translatorVersion` from tools/import-v1/translate-v1.js and never uses it (the L row the header promised, "the translator's name is @13", doesn't exist; see 624).
- client/lib/sky/sky_baked.js:59 `bakedCloudsPinned` is exported but nothing in the repo reads it any more (main's only reader was tools/sky-interim-probe.mjs); a dead export (see 585).
- client/lib/ragdoll/ammodoll-dressing.js:119 and :238 also pass an untracked `new AMMO.btVector3(...)` to btBoxShape, the same leak as 590; the header claim at client/lib/ragdoll/ammodoll.js:17 covers those files too.
- client/lib/ragdoll/ragdoll.js:505-512: nothing re-pins after the iterations, so `_terrain()` (:580) can lift a pinned joint off its nail for the rest of the substep (the behaviour behind 604's orphan comment; on main too).
- services/chatbridge/index.ts:150-152: the offline break skips the MAX_BACKLOG trim, so toWorld is unbounded during an outage (539; on main too). A 2-line move of the trim above the joined check fixes it.
- test/harness/cdp-pw.mjs:229: `context().newCDPSession()` returns only `{ send }`. Any probe written for Playwright's CDPSession events (`on`/`once`) breaks on the cdp-pw driver (577 is one).
- test/protocol/agentshove-check.ts:106 anyPunt matches refused requests by `"<id>"` in the body, but a refused request's body carries argNames only (shared/protocol/authority.js:252), so it can't see any refused punt, contact or not (556 fixes the label; the helper's own comment-free name still suggests it can).
- server/procgroup.ts:89-90: the killSuiteAsync doc ("killSuite, without blocking: ...") sits above winKillPlan's doc, detached from killSuiteAsync (:104), so editors and hovers attach neither correctly. Same on main :88-89.
- test/client/capsule-recovery-test.mjs:20-24: once #700's header edit lands, the RETRY_MAX bullet ("not distinguishable inside a sane test window … Measured 09-19") still sits under it and contradicts the cap section at :110-118. Delete those 5 lines (left out of the edit to keep it small).
- client/lib/audio/audiopanel.js:35/:172: `import { CONFIG }` exists only to pass `CONFIG.name` to `toggleMic()`, which takes no parameter (micstate.js:140). That's dead code: 2 product lines.
- client/lib/audio/micstate.js:19: `import { audioContext } from './audioctx.js'` is unused (#760). Dropping it may change audioctx.js's load order, so check before removing.
- mcpl/agent.ts:384-385 (#697): q2's dispositions (#281) say it corrected this doc's frame lists, but hep/333-review-q3 still has the short lists. If q2's branch lands first, drop the #697 edit.
- test/client/grass-quality-test.ts:24/:63/:183/:210: "both trees" still appears in a comment and two printed labels after #782. The A/B against main's tree needs main's client/lib/flora.js path.
- client/lib/ui/mention-rx.js:21 (#737): the empty-names fallback `'\\u0000'` in group 2 is the same NUL hole as the links:false one, so a NUL renders as a mention. It is pre-existing (main client/lib/mention-rx.js:20). One `'(?!)'` fixes both.
- client/lib/render/bodygate.js:23-27 (#685): a timeout never opens the gate, so every later world load waits its own 12 s while the body hangs. That is a behaviour question for the owner, not a comment.
- test/mcpl/door-cap-gate-live-test.mjs:48/:187 (#707): the PR moved it from kernel-assigned ports (main: listen(0)) to random freePort windows. harness.ts:31 kernelPort() would restore the kernel's pick in 3 call sites.

## Proposed, not applied

- **558, 559** tools/import-v1/translate-v1.js DROPPED: the drafted entries are in the a-lane edit list (local notes, not kept). Run test/protocol/translate-check.ts with them: the report hash at :316 includes DROPPED.
- **560** tools/import-v1/world.ts bindAdvice: prefix `WORLDS_DIR=<worlds dir> `, and move the expected strings in test/protocol/owners-check.ts BN3 (:283) and OB2 (:125) with it.
- **577** test/harness/cdp-pw.mjs:229: `newCDPSession` returns `{ send, once: (ev, fn) => { const off = C.listen((m) => { if (m.sessionId === S && m.method === ev) { off(); fn(m.params); } }); } }`. Race test/client/xr-quad-colour-probe.mjs:307's `activeSession?.end()` against `sleep(5000)`. Check: force `ok = false` after the poll; old: TypeError at `cdp.once`, new: the paused stack. Needs a browser.
- **700** test/client/capsule-recovery-test.mjs:20-24: delete the RETRY_MAX bullet that the new header contradicts (see Also spotted).

# #333 review, findings 796-1061: dispositions (head ded52c8fb, branch hep/333-review-q4)

Base for "pre-existing" is main f91058158 (`anima/main`). The labels mean the same as in q1-q3. "fix (test, red→green shown)": the strengthened row ran green, then red under the named mutant (the old row green under the same mutant), then green again. Counts are passed/failed of the suite. "fix (product, one-liner)": a small bug where main's stated intent wasn't met, each in its own commit (droppable). "PROPOSED": not applied, for the reason in the row.

Commits (on acd9ed487): b0fa9f743 ttslist empty-list loading-row check (#851) · 84fce2d22 base.js account lines through the wrapped store (#805) · 533b93251 boot bar floors its percent (#807) · 6acb508e1 particles-shots drops the unread WARM (#1033) · 73aaafed3 tests that couldn't fail · 7cb360e78 test row labels and printed lines (strings only, droppable) · a33f21fc9 server.ts imports OPT_DIR once (Also spotted) · 718f0cced comments inside embedded code (a shader string, page-evaluated test code; they change string bytes, so kept apart) · 3be5d39ff comments and docs (every code file transpiles to its parent's bytes).

Triage: two read-only helpers drafted rows and exact edits (796-928, 929-1061). I applied, verified and ran everything, and rewrote the rows whose outcome changed (994, 1057, 977, 937, 938, 1008).

Suites run after the last commit (each alone under the test lock, BUN_RUNTIME_TRANSPILER_CACHE_PATH=0): the four tree scanners (nonumber, paths 8/0, vocab, hygiene 33/0), and 56 suites that read the changed sources or carry the changed rows, all green except translate-check V7 (58/1), which is red at acd9ed487 too (Also spotted). Not run: the full gate, browser probes, and anything needing a KTX2 encoder.

## Also spotted (not in the bot's list; not fixed unless noted)

- **server/server.ts:18 and :49 both imported `OPT_DIR` from config.ts. FIXED in a33f21fc9** (the second import is `{ PATCH_DIR }` now). Bun's runtime forgives the duplicate binding, but Bun.Transpiler (and other engines) refuse the module, which is the class test/mcpl/body-state-bundle-test.ts guards for mcpl/. Same pair on main :20/:62. A strict parse of every tracked .js/.mjs/.ts outside vendor/ is clean after the fix.
- **Ten Chromium probes call `pg.waitForTimeout`, which the cdp-pw page doesn't have**: test/client/{library-badge,sky-interim,avatar-perf,rain-splash,ground-now,lite-banner,htmlmesh-wrap,gputime,xr-panel-drag,xr-dropdown}-probe.mjs (sky-interim dies at its first wait: "pg.waitForTimeout is not a function"). Main's tools/probe-harness.mjs launched Playwright's chromium; this branch's test/harness/probe-harness.mjs:26 uses launchCdp. All ten are in test/gate/ungated.json already ("fixed waits to turn into conditions"), so nothing gated breaks, but they can't run as they stand (introduced by the harness move; same family as q3's newCDPSession note).
- tools/water/README.md:73 said twenty-nine creates; the importer makes thirty (the submarine's passenger socket). Fixed with 1057's test. Same on main.
- `bun install` in mcpl/ rewrites mcpl/bun.lock here (typescript 5.9.3 → 7.0.2, from the pinned mcpl-core-ts sibling's devDependencies): the committed lock doesn't match the sibling the gate pins. Left uncommitted in the worktree.
- test/client/ttsrow-test.mjs has no row for the empty-list loading-row case 851 fixes; the fix is unpinned.
- test/protocol/translate-check.ts V7 ("a hull this host measures (the repo's ball.glb) … the walls are set off by the ball's box") is red here at this lane's base acd9ed487 too (floor -10, ceil 9: the walls aren't offset), so not from this lane; probably the host's measurer. Not investigated.
- mcpl/agent.ts:1218: the removedUntil field's comment carried a second, stray `// entity id -> registered box ids` (another field's comment, pasted after it). Dropped in #884's edit.
- test/client/flight-test.ts:874: "THREE deliberate importers" but ALLOWED (:879) lists four (agent.ts, agent-flight.ts, controller.js, physics.ts); the agent-flight split added one. Not edited.
- shared/protocol/authority/scriptrun.js:252: the `{deferred}` record's runtime `why` says the lifts "settle at the next load", but orphanReturns settles them in the same call (see #909). A string fix; left for the lead.
- tools/import-v1/fold.js:1-6 (#919): three stale pointers, unfixable without changing v1FoldBuild. The paths-check exemption for tools/import-v1/ (test/gate/paths-check.ts:20) is why paths-check never flagged `tools/protocol/nov1-check.ts`.
- client/lib/realize/texts.js:5-6 still says "Each paragraph's direction is its first strong letter's"; with #839's note in shared/text.js that's close enough, but the same digit caveat applies.
- client/lib/audio/engine-piper.js:2 (header) still lists "the OPFS cache seeding" as piper-specific work; true, but after #820 it reads as vestigial (see Proposed).
- client/lib/edit/widgets.js:177: `w: Math.max(28, 16)` is a constant 28 written as a max; harmless, and on main too.
- mcpl/agent-reach.ts:308: a failed reading (`.catch`) clears every joined reach's `reached`, so one transient read error fires release edges for all of them (behind #883).
- test/protocol/txn-check.ts:390 (T10): the tryUntil label is `'the reply to "operator-repair visitor"'`, copied from T9f; it waits for 100 replies. Suggest `'the 100 replies'`. Not drafted (it's printed only on a timeout).
- test/protocol/shiplist-check.ts:51: the suite's own mutant "Add Openable brings its open action" names O7, but O7 as it stands can't go red under it (#1040); `--mutants` should be reporting that today. The #1040 edit makes it hold.
- test/shared/components-corpus-test.ts:55 "(design §6)" and test/shared/component-needs-test.ts:2 "(design §3, step 3)": DESIGN.md has no numbered sections (its headings are named). Same stale-section class as #992.
- test/client/snap-at-probe.mjs:5 cites (local notes), which isn't in the repo (the same class as #985/#1047; paths-check doesn't see it because notes/ isn't a top-level dir).
- test/labs/particles-shots.ts:20: the default SHOTS dir is `<repo>/notes/particle-shots/<mode>`, so a bare run writes into the checkout (notes/ isn't tracked or ignored there; check before a run).
- test/client/voicesource-real-test.mjs:26-29: the core.js mock is dead (#1056); a mock of a module nobody imports passes silently whatever it returns.
- test/protocol/blockers-mutants.ts: no M9 row (#1022). If the architecture note's M9 has code, its mutant is missing; if it hasn't, M10's exemption note should name it too.

## Proposed, not applied

- **977** test/server/ktx2-transfer-test.ts: compare all four channels per quadrant (r = g = v, b = 255 − v, a = 255) at ≤ 6; mutant server/optimize.ts `"--generate-mipmap",` → `"--generate-mipmap", "--swizzle", "rrr1",`. Needs a host with toktx/ktx; run it unmutated first (ETC1S chroma may need more than 6 on b).
- **937, 938** test/client/sky-interim-probe.mjs: parse N from "world settled after N s" and require N < 59.5 (the 60 s cap logs the same line); `lines.length = 0` before the reload. Mutants: sky.js `if (bootDone() && !loadingBusy())` → `if (false)`; a reload-only skip of the "world settled" tee. Needs the probe's waits moved off `pg.waitForTimeout` first.
- **1008 / 919** tools/import-v1/fold.js: its stale pointers and the remove-branch comment, in a commit that means to move v1FoldBuild.
- #799: make the rebuild hook republish a synth fallback: export `sfuMicSynthetic = () => !!micStream?.synthetic` from client/lib/audio/voicesfu.js and guard `if (sfuMicOn() && !sfuMicSynthetic()) return;` in voicesfubridge.js:21. Pre-existing; needs a probe.
- #804: client/lib/render/net_capture.js:118: `const was = root.visible;` before the call and `setOwnVisible: (v) => { root.visible = v && was; }`, so an already-hidden root stays hidden. 2 lines.
- #805: setName (client/lib/base.js:123, :126) still calls `store?.setItem` raw; on a store whose calls throw, a rename throws. remember('ew-name', …) / remember('ew-account', …) there too (2 lines).
- #820: drop the OPFS seeding and PATH_MAP (client/lib/audio/engine-piper.js:59-83) and the `getDirectory` gate in `supported` (:19), since the hand-built session never reads them. Pre-existing; behaviour (a browser without OPFS would gain Piper), the owner's call.
- #827: whether getUp should keep the body's y (drop `myState.pos.y = 0`, client/lib/player/localbody.js:80) depends on the controller's fall/ground snap from a raised rest: unsure.
- #833: mark the engine failed when its constructor throws (in makeRagdoll's catch, client/lib/ragdoll/bodysim.js:80) so bodyEngine() says "running verlet". 1 line, but it would also stop later falls from retrying Bullet.
- #839: exclude the Arabic-Indic digit ranges from RTL in shared/text.js:52 (e.g. a `(?![٠-٩۰-۹])` lookahead before the class). One regex; needs a text-test row.
- #849: a chat line in stt.js's start() catch (client/lib/audio/stt.js:134): `const m = \`captions could not start: ${e?.message ?? e}\`; flashHint(m); logChat('*', m);`. Never on main.
- #852: call tick()/tickRow() after the row is appended (client/lib/audio/ttslist.js:165 after :167; :206 after the row lands in the pane), so the import clocks run. Pre-existing.
- #853/#854: tts-chunk spokenForm (client/lib/audio/tts-chunk.js:27, :31): add `\p{Regional_Indicator}` (and keycap U+20E3, tags U+E0020-E007F) to the emoji strip, and match `~~~` fences with a backreference `/(```|~~~)[\s\S]*?(?:\1|$)/g`. Each one regex; test/client/spoken-form-test.mjs rows.
- #884: a kick "may come back at once" (moderation.js:72), yet a kicked agent stays down (mcpl/agent.ts:606-614). Whether a kicked host should redial (after a short backoff) is behaviour: the owner's call.
- #927: faint (#5e5e66) as placeholder text on well is 2.91:1; lifting it to ≥4.5:1 (about #8a8a94) and adding ["faint", "well"] to test/client/edittheme-test.ts F would make the gap testable. A design call.
- #943: narrow the probes' console filter from any "401" to the signed-out /whoami fetch, via `m.location().url` in the collectors (test/client/xr-pointer-probe.mjs, test/client/xr-plate-card-probe.mjs). Needs someone who knows which 401s a probe page expects.
- #982: store-variants-test's freePort trusts a failed HTTP fetch; test/harness/harness.ts:32's freePort (or a bind-and-close probe) would be real. Small, but it touches every server section of that file.
- #995: boot-check could fail when the dock can't open the action bar (`no action bar dock button` / `click did not open it`), except on spectate/XR boots; unsure which boots legitimately lack the button.
- #1014: add SIGHUP to armSignals (test/harness/probe-harness.mjs:47), one word, if a closed terminal should sweep scratch servers too.
- #1042: drive frames reach the authority through messages.ts requestSync, outside requestwire's queue and meters; either route them through requestwire or widen T11 to name the exception. Design call.
- #978: a malformed attest (non-integer seq, empty digest) is dropped with no frame (server/messages.ts:243); answering it in words like the others is a two-line product change, the owner's call.
- To-dos for rows that don't exist: a nowhere-to-stand navgrid row (#979), a duplicate-caption receipt row in server-check CB (#1039), D40b's 24-char positive / 23-char negative (#1026), whether foldparity FP2 should sweep spec/conformance/physics/ (#1027), sky-flip coverage lost with its probe (#1048), the zero-intensity lamp lifecycle executed rather than grepped (#1055).
